### `entity` (type)

Base of every drawing entity.

- `ID`: unique within the drawing, assigned once, never reused; ascending ids define stacking order.
- `LAYER-ID`: id of the layer the entity belongs to.
- `BOUNDS`: world-space bounds, computed once at construction.

### `entity-area` (function)

```lisp
(entity-area entity)
```

Return the area that an entity encloses, in square millimetres: that of a closed shape, and what is left of a hatch
once its holes are taken out; 0 for any other.

- `ENTITY`: an entity.

### `entity-bounds` (function)

```lisp
(entity-bounds instance)
```

(undocumented)

### `entity-derived-values` (generic function)

```lisp
(entity-derived-values entity)
```

Return the read-only derived quantities of ENTITY as DERIVED-VALUE values.

- `ENTITY`: an entity.

### `entity-explode-pieces` (function)

```lisp
(entity-explode-pieces entity)
```

Return the entities that ENTITY comes apart into when it is exploded, where they were, or NIL when this kind of
entity cannot be exploded: a block, a rectangle, a polygon and a polyline can.

- `ENTITY`: an entity.

### `entity-grips` (generic function)

```lisp
(entity-grips entity)
```

Return the grips of ENTITY, the handles that the user drags to change it, as a list
of GRIP values. The default is none.

- `ENTITY`: an entity.

### `entity-id` (function)

```lisp
(entity-id instance)
```

(undocumented)

### `entity-kind` (generic function)

```lisp
(entity-kind entity)
```

Return the entity's kind keyword (:line, :rectangle, :circle, :polyline,
:text, :dimension, :arc, :ellipse, :polygon, :radial-dimension, :angular-dimension, :hatch or :insert).

- `ENTITY`: an entity.

### `entity-kinds` (function)

```lisp
(entity-kinds)
```

Return the list of entity kind keywords.

### `entity-layer-id` (function)

```lisp
(entity-layer-id instance)
```

(undocumented)

### `entity-length` (function)

```lisp
(entity-length entity)
```

Return the length of an entity that is a line, a curve, a polyline or a shape, in millimetres, the perimeter for a
shape that is closed; 0 for any other.

- `ENTITY`: an entity.

### `entity-mirrored` (generic function)

```lisp
(entity-mirrored entity line-x1 line-y1 line-x2 line-y2)
```

Return ENTITY reflected in the line through two points. A text is not turned back to front:
only its insertion point is reflected.

- `ENTITY`: an entity.
- `LINE-X1`: x of the first point of the line.
- `LINE-Y1`: y of the first point.
- `LINE-X2`: x of the second point.
- `LINE-Y2`: y of the second point.

### `entity-offset` (generic function)

```lisp
(entity-offset entity distance)
```

Return the entity that lies DISTANCE from ENTITY, with the same id and layer, or NIL when this kind
of entity cannot be offset. A positive distance grows a circle, an arc, a rectangle or a polygon, and a closed
polyline, and puts a line or an open polyline on its left, looking from its start to its end; a negative one
does the opposite. Signals INVALID-ENTITY when the result would not be valid.

- `ENTITY`: an entity.
- `DISTANCE`: millimetres.

### `entity-offset-through` (generic function)

```lisp
(entity-offset-through entity x y)
```

Return the signed distance that offsetting ENTITY must be given for the result to pass through the
point, on the side the offset uses, or NIL when this kind of entity cannot be offset. For a shape made of
segments it is the distance to the line of the nearest segment.

- `ENTITY`: an entity.
- `X`: world x of the point.
- `Y`: world y of the point.

### `entity-p` (function)

```lisp
(entity-p object)
```

(undocumented)

### `entity-path` (function)

```lisp
(entity-path entity)
```

Return the path that an entity makes, as two values: a flat array of its vertices, and true when the path is
closed. A curve is cut into many short segments. Returns NIL for an entity that is not a line, a polyline, an arc, a
circle, an ellipse, a rectangle or a polygon.

- `ENTITY`: an entity.

### `entity-pick-distance` (generic function)

```lisp
(entity-pick-distance entity x y)
```

Return the distance in millimetres from the point to the entity's selectable
geometry (outline for shapes, filled box for text); zero when the point is on it.

- `ENTITY`: an entity.
- `X`: point x.
- `Y`: point y.

### `entity-properties` (generic function)

```lisp
(entity-properties entity)
```

Return the inspector rows of ENTITY as a list of PROPERTY values.

- `ENTITY`: an entity.

### `entity-rotated` (generic function)

```lisp
(entity-rotated entity center-x center-y angle)
```

Return ENTITY turned about a point.

- `ENTITY`: an entity.
- `CENTER-X`: x of the center of the turn.
- `CENTER-Y`: y of the center.
- `ANGLE`: radians, counter-clockwise.

### `entity-scaled` (generic function)

```lisp
(entity-scaled entity center-x center-y factor)
```

Return ENTITY scaled about a point: every length of it, a text height included, is multiplied by
FACTOR.

- `ENTITY`: an entity.
- `CENTER-X`: x of the center of the scaling.
- `CENTER-Y`: y of the center.
- `FACTOR`: a positive number.

### `entity-snap-points` (generic function)

```lisp
(entity-snap-points entity)
```

Return the object-snap candidates of ENTITY as a list of (x y kind) lists,
where kind is :endpoint or :center.

- `ENTITY`: an entity.

### `entity-store` (type)

All entities of a drawing, ordered by id.

- `TABLE`: id to entity.
- `VECTOR`: entities sorted by ascending id; only the first COUNT slots are meaningful.
- `COUNT`: number of entities.

### `entity-touches-bounds-p` (generic function)

```lisp
(entity-touches-bounds-p entity bounds)
```

Return true when the selectable geometry of ENTITY has a point inside BOUNDS or on
its edge, which is what a crossing selection asks: an outline passing through the window touches it,
a shape that holds the whole window without its outline reaching it does not. The default is that the
bounds of the entity overlap BOUNDS, right for shapes that are filled boxes.

- `ENTITY`: an entity.
- `BOUNDS`: the window, in world coordinates.

### `entity-translated` (generic function)

```lisp
(entity-translated entity dx dy)
```

Return a copy of ENTITY moved by (DX, DY), with the same id.

- `ENTITY`: an entity.
- `DX`: displacement along x.
- `DY`: displacement along y.

### `entity-with-grip` (generic function)

```lisp
(entity-with-grip entity grip x y)
```

Return a copy of ENTITY, with the same id, changed as the dragging of GRIP to the
world point (X, Y) asks. Signals INVALID-ENTITY when the result would be invalid.

- `ENTITY`: an entity.
- `GRIP`: one of the grips returned by ENTITY-GRIPS.
- `X`: world x the grip is dragged to.
- `Y`: world y the grip is dragged to.

### `entity-with-identity` (generic function)

```lisp
(entity-with-identity entity id layer-id)
```

Return a copy of ENTITY with another id on another layer, for an entity that is
pasted as a new one.

- `ENTITY`: an entity.
- `ID`: the new id, unused in the drawing it goes into.
- `LAYER-ID`: id of the layer it goes onto.

### `entity-with-layer` (generic function)

```lisp
(entity-with-layer entity layer-id)
```

Return a copy of ENTITY on another layer, with the same id.

- `ENTITY`: an entity.
- `LAYER-ID`: id of the destination layer.

### `entity-with-property` (generic function)

```lisp
(entity-with-property entity key value)
```

Return a copy of ENTITY with one property changed, validated by the entity's
constructor. Signals INVALID-ENTITY when the result would be invalid.

- `ENTITY`: an entity.
- `KEY`: a PROPERTY-KEY returned by ENTITY-PROPERTIES.
- `VALUE`: new value (double-float, string, boolean or unit keyword according to the kind).

### `existing-editable-entities` (function)

```lisp
(existing-editable-entities drawing ids)
```

Return two values: the entities among IDS that exist and are editable, and how many were
skipped because they are missing or on a locked layer.

- `DRAWING`: the drawing.
- `IDS`: list of entity ids.

### `explode-changes` (function)

```lisp
(explode-changes drawing ids)
```

Return the change set that replaces each block, rectangle, polygon and polyline among the entities with IDS by the
entities it comes apart into (the sides of a shape, the entities of a placement of a block), where they were and on
its layer, and how many entities were exploded and how many of IDS were skipped (missing, on a locked layer, or of
a kind that does not come apart).

- `DRAWING`: the drawing.
- `IDS`: list of entity ids.

### `extend-changes` (function)

```lisp
(extend-changes drawing id x y)
```

Return the change set that extends the line or the arc with ID, at the end that is nearer to the point, up to the
first entity that stands in its way. Signals EDIT-REFUSED when it cannot be done.

- `DRAWING`: the drawing.
- `ID`: id of the entity to extend.
- `X`: world x of the click, which says which end.
- `Y`: world y of the click.

### `extract-entities` (function)

```lisp
(extract-entities drawing entities)
```

Return a new drawing that holds ENTITIES, with the ids and layer ids they have, and the layers
they are on, with the settings of DRAWING that give its numbers meaning.

- `DRAWING`: the drawing the entities come from.
- `ENTITIES`: a non-empty list of entities of DRAWING.

### `fillet-changes` (function)

```lisp
(fillet-changes drawing first-id first-x first-y second-id second-x second-y radius)
```

Return the change set that rounds the corner of two lines: each is cut or lengthened to the point where an arc of
the given radius touches it, on the side of the corner where it was clicked, and the arc is added on the layer of the
first. With a radius of zero the lines are only taken to the corner. Signals EDIT-REFUSED when it cannot be done.

- `DRAWING`: the drawing.
- `FIRST-ID`: id of the first line.
- `FIRST-X`: world x of the click on the first line.
- `FIRST-Y`: world y of the click on the first line.
- `SECOND-ID`: id of the second line.
- `SECOND-X`: world x of the click on the second line.
- `SECOND-Y`: world y of the click on the second line.
- `RADIUS`: radius of the arc, millimetres, zero or more.

### `find-block` (function)

```lisp
(find-block drawing name)
```

Return the block called NAME, compared without regard to case, or NIL.

- `DRAWING`: the drawing.
- `NAME`: a block name.

### `find-layer` (function)

```lisp
(find-layer drawing id)
```

Return the layer with ID, or NIL.

- `DRAWING`: the drawing.
- `ID`: layer id.

### `find-layer-by-name` (function)

```lisp
(find-layer-by-name drawing name)
```

Return the layer called NAME (compared case-insensitively), or NIL.

- `DRAWING`: the drawing.
- `NAME`: layer name.

### `find-object-snap` (function)

```lisp
(find-object-snap drawing x y radius &optional anchor)
```

Return the object-snap point nearest to (X, Y) within RADIUS as three values (x, y, kind), or NIL. The
candidates, on the pickable entities near the pointer, are their endpoints and centers, the midpoints of their
segments and arcs, the quadrants of circles and arcs, the points where they cross, and, when there is an
anchor, the feet of perpendiculars to them from it and the points where a line from it touches a circle or an
arc. At equal distances the endpoint wins, then the intersection, the center, the midpoint, the quadrant, the
perpendicular and the tangent.

- `DRAWING`: the drawing.
- `X`: point x in millimetres.
- `Y`: point y in millimetres.
- `RADIUS`: snap radius in millimetres.
- `ANCHOR`: an (x . y) cons, the last point drawn, or NIL.

### `grip` (type)

A handle on an entity that the user can drag to change it.

- `ID`: identifies the grip among the grips of its entity, for ENTITY-WITH-GRIP.
- `X`: world x of the grip.
- `Y`: world y of the grip.
- `KIND`: :point for a grip that changes the shape (an end, a corner, a radius), :move for the grip that
moves the whole entity.

### `grip-changes` (function)

```lisp
(grip-changes drawing id grip x y)
```

Return the change set that drags a grip of one entity to a point, or NIL when that leaves the
entity as it is. Signals LAYER-ERROR when the entity is missing or locked, and INVALID-ENTITY when
the result would be invalid.

- `DRAWING`: the drawing.
- `ID`: entity id.
- `GRIP`: one of the grips of the entity, from ENTITY-GRIPS.
- `X`: world x the grip is dragged to.
- `Y`: world y the grip is dragged to.

### `grip-id` (function)

```lisp
(grip-id instance)
```

(undocumented)

### `grip-kind` (function)

```lisp
(grip-kind instance)
```

(undocumented)

### `grip-x` (function)

```lisp
(grip-x instance)
```

(undocumented)

### `grip-y` (function)

```lisp
(grip-y instance)
```

(undocumented)

### `hatch-angle` (function)

```lisp
(hatch-angle instance)
```

(undocumented)

### `hatch-changes` (function)

```lisp
(hatch-changes drawing x y &key (pattern lines) (angle +default-hatch-angle+) spacing)
```

Return the change set that adds a hatch of the smallest closed shape that holds a point, on the current layer.
Signals EDIT-REFUSED when no closed shape holds it, and INVALID-ENTITY when the hatch would not be valid.

- `DRAWING`: the drawing.
- `X`: world x of the point.
- `Y`: world y of the point.
- `PATTERN`: :solid, :lines or :cross.
- `ANGLE`: direction of the lines, in radians.
- `SPACING`: distance between two lines, or NIL for the default of the region.

### `hatch-coordinates` (function)

```lisp
(hatch-coordinates instance)
```

(undocumented)

### `hatch-entity` (type)

A region filled with a pattern: solid, or lines at an angle and a spacing, or two sets of them at right angles. The
lines are cut by the boundary, and one of them passes through the first vertex, so the pattern moves with the
region.

- `COORDINATES`: flat array x0 y0 x1 y1 ... of the vertices of the boundary, a simple polygon; owned by the entity.
- `HOLES`: a list of such arrays, polygons inside the boundary that the pattern leaves unfilled; owned by the entity.
- `PATTERN`: :solid, :lines or :cross.
- `ANGLE`: direction of the lines, in radians, kept in [0, pi) since a line has no sense.
- `SPACING`: distance between two lines, in millimetres.
- `SEGMENTS`: the lines, as a flat array x1 y1 x2 y2 ..., computed at construction; empty for a solid hatch.
- `TRIANGLES`: the triangles that cover the region, as a flat array of six numbers each, computed at construction; empty
unless the hatch is solid.

### `hatch-hole-coordinates` (function)

```lisp
(hatch-hole-coordinates hatch)
```

Return the vertices of all the holes of a hatch one after the other, as a file stores them.

- `HATCH`: a hatch entity.

### `hatch-hole-sizes` (function)

```lisp
(hatch-hole-sizes hatch)
```

Return how many vertices each hole of a hatch has, as an array of numbers.

- `HATCH`: a hatch entity.

### `hatch-holes` (function)

```lisp
(hatch-holes instance)
```

(undocumented)

### `hatch-pattern` (function)

```lisp
(hatch-pattern instance)
```

(undocumented)

### `hatch-region-at` (function)

```lisp
(hatch-region-at drawing x y)
```

Return the boundary of the smallest closed shape on a visible layer that holds a point, as a flat array of
vertices, or NIL when none does, and as a second value the boundaries of the closed shapes inside it that do not hold
the point, which are holes in what is hatched.

- `DRAWING`: the drawing.
- `X`: world x of the point.
- `Y`: world y of the point.

### `hatch-segments` (function)

```lisp
(hatch-segments instance)
```

(undocumented)

### `hatch-spacing` (function)

```lisp
(hatch-spacing instance)
```

(undocumented)

### `hatch-triangles` (function)

```lisp
(hatch-triangles instance)
```

(undocumented)

### `history` (type)

Undo and redo stacks of change sets.

- `UNDO-STACK`: applied change sets, most recent first.
- `REDO-STACK`: undone change sets, most recently undone first.
- `LIMIT`: most change sets kept in the undo stack.

### `history-can-redo-p` (function)

```lisp
(history-can-redo-p history)
```

Return true when there is something to redo.

- `HISTORY`: a history.

### `history-can-undo-p` (function)

```lisp
(history-can-undo-p history)
```

Return true when there is something to undo.

- `HISTORY`: a history.

### `history-clear` (function)

```lisp
(history-clear history)
```

Forget every undoable and redoable change set.

- `HISTORY`: the history.

### `history-commit` (function)

```lisp
(history-commit history drawing changes)
```

Apply CHANGES to DRAWING and record them for undo, discarding the redo stack. An empty
change set is ignored and returns NIL.

- `HISTORY`: the history to record into.
- `DRAWING`: the drawing to change.
- `CHANGES`: list of CHANGE records.

### `history-latest-step` (function)

```lisp
(history-latest-step history)
```

Return the change set done most recently and not undone, or NIL when there is none.

- `HISTORY`: the history.

### `history-record` (function)

```lisp
(history-record history changes)
```

Record CHANGES, which have already been applied to the drawing, for undo, discarding the redo
stack: what a command that made its changes one by one, to see them as it went, leaves as one
step. An empty change set is ignored and returns NIL.

- `HISTORY`: the history to record into.
- `CHANGES`: list of CHANGE records, each entity appearing at most once, that take the drawing from
how it was before the command to how it is now.

### `history-redo` (function)

```lisp
(history-redo history drawing)
```

Redo the most recently undone change set; return true, or NIL when there is nothing to redo.

- `HISTORY`: the history.
- `DRAWING`: the drawing to change.

### `history-redo-depth` (function)

```lisp
(history-redo-depth history)
```

Return the number of change sets that can be redone.

- `HISTORY`: a history.

### `history-steps-since` (function)

```lisp
(history-steps-since history step)
```

Return the change sets done after STEP, oldest first, as undo has left them: one that was undone is not in
them, and one that was done again is. The second value is true when they can be told, and NIL when STEP was
undone or forgotten, so that what came after it is not known.

- `HISTORY`: the history.
- `STEP`: a change set that was the latest at some time, or NIL for the beginning.

### `history-undo` (function)

```lisp
(history-undo history drawing)
```

Undo the most recent change set; return true, or NIL when there is nothing to undo.

- `HISTORY`: the history.
- `DRAWING`: the drawing to change.

### `history-undo-depth` (function)

```lisp
(history-undo-depth history)
```

Return the number of change sets that can be undone.

- `HISTORY`: a history.

### `insert-block` (function)

```lisp
(insert-block instance)
```

### `insert-changes` (function)

```lisp
(insert-changes drawing name x y &key (scale 1.0d0) (rotation 0.0d0) flipped)
```

Return the change set that places the block called NAME on the current layer. Signals EDIT-REFUSED when there is no
such block, and INVALID-ENTITY when the placement would not be valid.

- `DRAWING`: the drawing.
- `NAME`: the name of the block.
- `X`: x of the point the base point of the block goes to.
- `Y`: y of that point.
- `SCALE`: how many times larger than the definition.
- `ROTATION`: the turn, in radians.
- `FLIPPED`: true to reflect the block across its vertical axis first.

### `insert-entities` (function)

```lisp
(insert-entities instance)
```

(undocumented)

### `insert-entity` (type)

A block placed in a drawing.

- `BLOCK`: the block definition.
- `X`: x of the point where the base point of the block goes.
- `Y`: y of that point.
- `SCALE`: how many times larger than the definition it is.
- `ROTATION`: how far it is turned, in radians counter-clockwise, in [0, 2 pi).
- `FLIPPED`: true when the block is reflected across its own vertical axis before it is turned.
- `ENTITIES`: the entities of the block as placed, on the layer of the placement, computed at construction.

### `invert-changes` (function)

```lisp
(invert-changes changes)
```

Return the change set that undoes CHANGES.

- `CHANGES`: list of CHANGE records.

### `layer` (type)

A drawing layer. Layers are immutable; the drawing replaces them when they change.

- `ID`: unique, never reused within a drawing.
- `NAME`: non-empty, unique within the drawing.
- `COLOR`: RGB color applied to every entity on the layer.
- `VISIBLE`: when false the layer is neither drawn nor pickable.
- `LOCKED`: when true the layer's entities are drawn but cannot be picked or edited.


Return how many entities are on a layer.

- `DRAWING`: the drawing.
- `LAYER-ID`: layer id.

### `line-entity` (type)

A straight segment between two points.

- `X1`: x of the start point.
- `Y1`: y of the start point.
- `X2`: x of the end point.
- `Y2`: y of the end point.

### `make-angular-dimension` (function)

```lisp
(make-angular-dimension &key id layer-id vertex-x vertex-y start-angle end-angle radius text-height precision)
```

Create a validated angle dimension. Signals INVALID-ENTITY when an invariant is violated.

- `ID`: positive entity id.
- `LAYER-ID`: id of the layer.
- `VERTEX-X`: x of the vertex, in millimetres.
- `VERTEX-Y`: y of the vertex.
- `START-ANGLE`: direction of the first arm in radians; any finite angle, kept reduced to [0, 2 pi).
- `END-ANGLE`: direction of the second arm; the angle is measured counter-clockwise from the first arm, and the
two must differ.
- `RADIUS`: radius of the dimension arc, above the length tolerance.
- `TEXT-HEIGHT`: text height (em size), above the length tolerance.
- `PRECISION`: decimals displayed, 0 to +MAXIMUM-DIMENSION-PRECISION+.

### `make-arc` (function)

```lisp
(make-arc &key id layer-id center-x center-y radius start-angle end-angle)
```

Create a validated arc. Signals INVALID-ENTITY when an invariant is violated.

- `ID`: positive entity id.
- `LAYER-ID`: id of the layer.
- `CENTER-X`: x of the center, in millimetres.
- `CENTER-Y`: y of the center.
- `RADIUS`: radius.
- `START-ANGLE`: direction of the start in radians; any finite angle, kept reduced to [0, 2 pi).
- `END-ANGLE`: direction of the end; the arc turns counter-clockwise from the start to it, and the
two must differ.

### `make-block-changes` (function)

```lisp
(make-block-changes drawing ids name base-x base-y)
```

Return the definition of a block made of the entities with IDS, and the change set that replaces them with a
placement of it at the base point, on the layer of the first of them, and how many of IDS were skipped because they are
missing or on a locked layer. The caller adds the definition to the drawing before applying the changes. Signals
EDIT-REFUSED when there is nothing to make it of, the name is no good or is that of a block in use, or one of the
entities is a placement; and INVALID-ENTITY when the placement would not be valid.

- `DRAWING`: the drawing.
- `IDS`: list of entity ids, in the order they are to be drawn in the block.
- `NAME`: what the block is called.
- `BASE-X`: x of the base point of the block.
- `BASE-Y`: y of the base point.

### `make-block-definition` (function)

```lisp
(make-block-definition &key name entities (base-x 0.0d0) (base-y 0.0d0))
```

Create a block from entities that are in a drawing, moved so that the base point is the origin and given the ids 1
and on and the default layer. Signals EDIT-REFUSED when the name is no good, there are no entities, there are too many
of them, or one of them is a placement of a block, which a block cannot hold; and INVALID-ENTITY when moving one makes
it invalid.

- `NAME`: what the block is called.
- `ENTITIES`: the entities, in the order they are to be drawn.
- `BASE-X`: x of the point that is the origin of the block.
- `BASE-Y`: y of that point.

### `make-change` (function)

```lisp
(make-change entity-id before after)
```

### `make-circle` (function)

```lisp
(make-circle &key id layer-id center-x center-y radius)
```

Create a validated circle. Signals INVALID-ENTITY when an invariant is violated.

- `ID`: positive entity id.
- `LAYER-ID`: id of the layer.
- `CENTER-X`: x of the center, in millimetres.
- `CENTER-Y`: y of the center.
- `RADIUS`: radius.

### `make-derived-value` (function)

```lisp
(make-derived-value label kind value)
```

(undocumented)

### `make-dimension` (function)

```lisp
(make-dimension &key id layer-id x1 y1 x2 y2 offset text-height unit precision)
```

Create a validated aligned dimension. Signals INVALID-ENTITY when an invariant is violated.

- `ID`: positive entity id.
- `LAYER-ID`: id of the layer.
- `X1`: x of the first definition point, in millimetres.
- `Y1`: y of the first definition point.
- `X2`: x of the second definition point.
- `Y2`: y of the second definition point.
- `OFFSET`: signed distance of the dimension line from the measured segment.
- `TEXT-HEIGHT`: text height (em size), above the length tolerance.
- `UNIT`: displayed length unit keyword.
- `PRECISION`: decimals displayed, 0 to +MAXIMUM-DIMENSION-PRECISION+.

### `make-drawing` (function)

```lisp
(make-drawing)
```

Create an empty drawing with the default layer, millimetre units and default settings.

### `make-ellipse` (function)

```lisp
(make-ellipse &key id layer-id center-x center-y radius-x radius-y (rotation 0.0d0))
```

Create a validated ellipse. Signals INVALID-ENTITY when an invariant is violated.

- `ID`: positive entity id.
- `LAYER-ID`: id of the layer.
- `CENTER-X`: x of the center, in millimetres.
- `CENTER-Y`: y of the center.
- `RADIUS-X`: semi-axis in the direction ROTATION.
- `RADIUS-Y`: semi-axis across it.
- `ROTATION`: direction of the first axis in radians; any finite angle, kept reduced to [0, pi).

### `make-entity-store` (function)

```lisp
(make-entity-store)
```

(undocumented)

### `make-grip` (function)

```lisp
(make-grip id x y kind)
```

(undocumented)

### `make-hatch` (function)

```lisp
(make-hatch &key id layer-id coordinates holes hole-coordinates hole-sizes (pattern lines) (angle +default-hatch-angle+) spacing)
```

Create a validated hatch. Signals INVALID-ENTITY when an invariant is violated: a boundary of at least three and at
most +MAXIMUM-HATCH-VERTICES+ vertices, in range, that encloses an area, and holes of the same kind, all of them with
at most that many vertices together; a known pattern; and a spacing that is above the tolerance and does not make
more than +MAXIMUM-HATCH-LINES+ lines.

- `ID`: positive entity id.
- `LAYER-ID`: id of the layer.
- `COORDINATES`: sequence of doubles x0 y0 x1 y1 ... of the boundary; copied.
- `HOLES`: a list of such sequences, polygons the pattern leaves unfilled; copied.
- `HOLE-COORDINATES`: instead of HOLES, the vertices of all the holes one after the other, as a file stores them.
- `HOLE-SIZES`: with HOLE-COORDINATES, how many vertices each hole has.
- `PATTERN`: :solid, :lines or :cross.
- `ANGLE`: direction of the lines in radians; any finite angle, kept in [0, pi).
- `SPACING`: distance between two lines, in millimetres.

### `make-history` (function)

```lisp
(make-history &key (limit +history-depth-limit+))
```

(undocumented)

### `make-insert` (function)

```lisp
(make-insert &key id layer-id block x y (scale 1.0d0) (rotation 0.0d0) flipped)
```

Create a validated placement of a block. Signals INVALID-ENTITY when an invariant is violated, including when
placing the block makes one of its entities invalid, for example by taking it out of the drawing.

- `ID`: positive entity id.
- `LAYER-ID`: id of the layer.
- `BLOCK`: the block definition.
- `X`: x of the point where the base point goes, in millimetres.
- `Y`: y of that point.
- `SCALE`: how many times larger, above zero.
- `ROTATION`: the turn in radians; any finite angle, kept reduced to [0, 2 pi).
- `FLIPPED`: true to reflect the block across its vertical axis before it is turned.

### `make-line` (function)

```lisp
(make-line &key id layer-id x1 y1 x2 y2)
```

Create a validated line. Signals INVALID-ENTITY when an invariant is violated.

- `ID`: positive entity id.
- `LAYER-ID`: id of the layer.
- `X1`: x of the start point, in millimetres.
- `Y1`: y of the start point.
- `X2`: x of the end point.
- `Y2`: y of the end point.

### `make-polygon` (function)

```lisp
(make-polygon &key id layer-id center-x center-y radius sides (rotation 0.0d0))
```

Create a validated regular polygon. Signals INVALID-ENTITY when an invariant is violated.

- `ID`: positive entity id.
- `LAYER-ID`: id of the layer.
- `CENTER-X`: x of the center, in millimetres.
- `CENTER-Y`: y of the center.
- `RADIUS`: radius of the circle through the vertices.
- `SIDES`: number of sides, an integer from 3 to +MAXIMUM-POLYGON-SIDES+.
- `ROTATION`: direction of the first vertex in radians; any finite angle, kept reduced to [0, 2 pi).

### `make-polyline` (function)

```lisp
(make-polyline &key id layer-id coordinates closed)
```

Create a validated polyline. Signals INVALID-ENTITY when an invariant is violated: at least
two vertices (three when closed), at most +MAXIMUM-POLYLINE-VERTICES+, every vertex in range,
and no two consecutive vertices coincident (including last and first when closed).

- `ID`: positive entity id.
- `LAYER-ID`: id of the layer.
- `COORDINATES`: sequence of doubles x0 y0 x1 y1 ...; copied.
- `CLOSED`: true for a closed polyline.

### `make-radial-dimension` (function)

```lisp
(make-radial-dimension &key id layer-id center-x center-y radius angle diameter text-height unit precision)
```

Create a validated radius or diameter dimension. Signals INVALID-ENTITY when an invariant is violated.

- `ID`: positive entity id.
- `LAYER-ID`: id of the layer.
- `CENTER-X`: x of the center, in millimetres.
- `CENTER-Y`: y of the center.
- `RADIUS`: radius, above the length tolerance.
- `ANGLE`: direction of the leader in radians; any finite angle, kept reduced to [0, 2 pi).
- `DIAMETER`: true for a diameter dimension.
- `TEXT-HEIGHT`: text height (em size), above the length tolerance.
- `UNIT`: displayed length unit keyword.
- `PRECISION`: decimals displayed, 0 to +MAXIMUM-DIMENSION-PRECISION+.

### `make-rectangle` (function)

```lisp
(make-rectangle &key id layer-id min-x min-y width height)
```

Create a validated rectangle. Signals INVALID-ENTITY when an invariant is violated.

- `ID`: positive entity id.
- `LAYER-ID`: id of the layer.
- `MIN-X`: x of the left edge, in millimetres.
- `MIN-Y`: y of the bottom edge.
- `WIDTH`: extent along x.
- `HEIGHT`: extent along y.

### `make-rectangle-from-corners` (function)

```lisp
(make-rectangle-from-corners &key id layer-id x1 y1 x2 y2)
```

Create a rectangle from two opposite corners given in any order.

- `ID`: positive entity id.
- `LAYER-ID`: id of the layer.
- `X1`: x of the first corner.
- `Y1`: y of the first corner.
- `X2`: x of the opposite corner.
- `Y2`: y of the opposite corner.

```lisp

```

(undocumented)

### `make-sheet` (function)

```lisp
(make-sheet &key (name sheet) (paper a3) (landscape t) fields viewports)
```

Create a sheet. Signals EDIT-REFUSED, with the reason, when it makes no sense.

- `NAME`: what it is called, not empty.
- `PAPER`: a keyword of *SHEET-PAPERS*.
- `LANDSCAPE`: true for paper lying down.
- `FIELDS`: the title block, an alist from keys to text; the keys it lacks are empty.
- `VIEWPORTS`: list of viewports.

### `make-text` (function)

```lisp
(make-text &key id layer-id x y string height (angle 0.0d0))
```

Create a validated text annotation. Signals INVALID-ENTITY when an invariant is violated.

- `ID`: positive entity id.
- `LAYER-ID`: id of the layer.
- `X`: x of the insertion point, in millimetres.
- `Y`: y of the insertion point.
- `STRING`: non-empty single-line text without control characters; copied.
- `HEIGHT`: text height (em size), above the length tolerance.
- `ANGLE`: direction of the baseline in radians, counter-clockwise from +x; any finite angle, kept
reduced to [0, 2 pi).

### `make-viewport` (function)

```lisp
(make-viewport &key x y width height center-x center-y scale)
```

Create a viewport. Signals EDIT-REFUSED, with the reason, when it makes no sense.

- `X`: x of its lower left corner on the sheet.
- `Y`: y of that corner.
- `WIDTH`: width on the sheet, above zero.
- `HEIGHT`: height on the sheet, above zero.
- `CENTER-X`: x of the point of the drawing at its center.
- `CENTER-Y`: y of that point.
- `SCALE`: millimetres of paper for a millimetre of the drawing, above zero.

### `map-entities` (function)

```lisp
(map-entities function store)
```

Call FUNCTION on every entity in id order (bottom of the stacking order first).
FUNCTION must not add or remove entities.

- `FUNCTION`: a function of one argument, the entity.
- `STORE`: an entity store.

### `mirror-entities-changes` (function)

```lisp
(mirror-entities-changes drawing ids line-x1 line-y1 line-x2 line-y2 &key copy)
```

Return the change set that reflects the entities with IDS in the line through two points (or adds reflected
copies), and the number skipped.

- `DRAWING`: the drawing.
- `IDS`: list of entity ids.
- `LINE-X1`: x of the first point of the line.
- `LINE-Y1`: y of the first point.
- `LINE-X2`: x of the second point.
- `LINE-Y2`: y of the second point.
- `COPY`: true to keep the originals and add the reflected copies.

### `mirror-xy` (function)

```lisp
(mirror-xy x y line-x1 line-y1 line-x2 line-y2)
```

Return, as two values, the reflection of the point (X, Y) in the line through two points.

- `X`: x of the point.
- `Y`: y of the point.
- `LINE-X1`: x of the first point of the line.
- `LINE-Y1`: y of the first point.
- `LINE-X2`: x of the second point.
- `LINE-Y2`: y of the second point.

### `offset-changes` (function)

```lisp
(offset-changes drawing id distance)
```

Return the change set that adds the entity that lies DISTANCE from the entity with ID, on the same layer.
Signals EDIT-REFUSED when the entity is missing, on a locked layer or of a kind that cannot be offset, and
INVALID-ENTITY when the result would not be valid.

- `DRAWING`: the drawing.
- `ID`: id of the entity to offset.
- `DISTANCE`: millimetres; positive grows or goes left, see ENTITY-OFFSET.

### `palette-color-after` (function)

```lisp
(palette-color-after color)
```

Return the palette color that follows COLOR, or the first when COLOR is not in the palette.

- `COLOR`: an RGB color.

### `palette-color-at` (function)

```lisp
(palette-color-at index)
```

Return the palette color at INDEX, counting round the palette: the color a new layer is given.

- `INDEX`: a whole number, such as the id the next layer will have.

### `pasted-entities` (function)

```lisp
(pasted-entities drawing snippet dx dy)
```

Return the entities of SNIPPET as new entities of DRAWING: with fresh ids, on the layers that
PASTE-LAYER-ID picks, and moved by (DX, DY). The blocks of SNIPPET that DRAWING does not have are added to it, and a
placement of a block that it has uses its block, whatever the copy said.

- `DRAWING`: the drawing they are pasted into.
- `SNIPPET`: the copied drawing.
- `DX`: displacement along x.
- `DY`: displacement along y.

### `path-array-changes` (function)

```lisp
(path-array-changes drawing ids path-id &key count spacing align)
```

Return the change set that lays copies of the entities with IDS along the path that another entity makes, the
originals being the item at the start of the path, and how many were skipped. With COUNT the items are at equal steps
from the start to the end of the path (round a closed one); with SPACING there is one every that distance, as many as
fit. Signals EDIT-REFUSED when the entity is missing or not a path, or when there are fewer than two items.

- `DRAWING`: the drawing.
- `IDS`: list of entity ids; the path itself is left out of them.
- `PATH-ID`: id of the line, polyline, arc, circle, ellipse, rectangle or polygon the items follow.
- `COUNT`: how many items in all, the originals included, or NIL to use SPACING.
- `SPACING`: distance between one item and the next along the path, in millimetres, or NIL to use COUNT.
- `ALIGN`: true to turn each copy to follow the direction of the path.

### `pick-entity` (function)

```lisp
(pick-entity drawing x y radius &optional kinds)
```

Return the pickable entity nearest to the point within RADIUS, or NIL. When several are at
the same distance the one created last (topmost) wins.

- `DRAWING`: the drawing.
- `X`: point x in millimetres.
- `Y`: point y in millimetres.
- `RADIUS`: pick radius in millimetres.
- `KINDS`: a list of entity kinds, when only entities of those kinds may be picked.

### `pickable-entities` (function)

```lisp
(pickable-entities drawing)
```

Return every pickable entity, in stacking order.

- `DRAWING`: the drawing.

### `pickable-p` (function)

```lisp
(pickable-p drawing entity)
```

Return true when ENTITY may be selected: its layer exists, is visible and is not locked.

- `DRAWING`: the drawing.
- `ENTITY`: an entity.

### `polar-array-changes` (function)

```lisp
(polar-array-changes drawing ids center-x center-y count total-angle &key (rotate-items t))
```

Return the change set that lays copies of the entities with IDS round a point, the originals being the first of COUNT
items, and how many were skipped. A total angle of a full turn or more puts the items at equal steps round the whole
circle; a smaller one puts the last item at the end of the angle. Signals EDIT-REFUSED when there are fewer than two
items or the angle is zero.

- `DRAWING`: the drawing.
- `IDS`: list of entity ids.
- `CENTER-X`: x of the center.
- `CENTER-Y`: y of the center.
- `COUNT`: how many items in all, the originals included, at least 2.
- `TOTAL-ANGLE`: the angle the items are spread over, in radians, counter-clockwise when positive.
- `ROTATE-ITEMS`: true to turn each copy as it goes round; false to keep it as it was drawn, only moved.

### `quantity-cell-text` (function)

```lisp
(quantity-cell-text value type unit &key (decimal-mark .))
```

Return a value of a report as text: a length or an area in the display unit, with one more decimal than is shown for
a length elsewhere.

- `VALUE`: the value, or NIL for a cell that is empty.
- `TYPE`: the type of its column, :text, :count, :length or :area.
- `UNIT`: the display unit.
- `DECIMAL-MARK`: the character between the whole part and the decimals.

### `quantity-heading` (function)


Return the heading of a column of a report with the unit of its numbers.

### `quantity-report` (function)

```lisp
(quantity-report drawing kind &key ids)
```

Return a report of what the drawing holds. Signals EDIT-REFUSED when there is nothing to count.

- `DRAWING`: the drawing.
- `KIND`: :layer for the totals by layer, :kind for those by kind of entity, :block for the blocks that are placed, or
:cut for the cut list.
- `IDS`: the ids of the entities to count, or NIL for everything on the layers that are not hidden.

### `quantity-report-csv` (function)

```lisp
(quantity-report-csv report unit &key (separator ,) (decimal-mark .))
```

Return a report as the text of a CSV file: a line of headings, a line for each row and one for the totals, with
lines that end in a carriage return and a line feed, as RFC 4180 has it.

- `REPORT`: the report.
- `UNIT`: the display unit, which the lengths and areas are written in.
- `SEPARATOR`: the character between fields; with a semicolon, and a comma as the decimal mark, Excel in Portuguese
opens it.
- `DECIMAL-MARK`: the character between the whole part and the decimals of a number.

### `radial-dimension-entity` (type)

A radius or diameter dimension of a circle or an arc: a leader from the center through the circle at an angle,
and the measured value. The value is derived from the radius; the text is derived from it, the unit and the
precision.

- `CENTER-X`: x of the center of the circle.
- `CENTER-Y`: y of the center of the circle.
- `RADIUS`: radius of the circle.
- `ANGLE`: direction of the leader from the center, in radians counter-clockwise from +x, in [0, 2 pi).
- `DIAMETER`: true for a diameter dimension, false for a radius dimension.
- `TEXT-HEIGHT`: text height (em size); every part of the dimension scales with it.
- `UNIT`: length unit keyword of the displayed text.
- `PRECISION`: decimals in the displayed text.
- `TEXT`: the displayed text, computed at construction.
- `LAYOUT`: the geometric layout of all parts, computed at construction.

### `rectangular-array-changes` (function)

```lisp
(rectangular-array-changes drawing ids rows columns row-spacing column-spacing &key (angle 0.0d0))
```

Return the change set that lays copies of the entities with IDS in a grid, the originals being its first item, and
how many were skipped. Signals EDIT-REFUSED when the grid has one item or the spacing of a direction that has more is
zero.

- `DRAWING`: the drawing.
- `IDS`: list of entity ids.
- `ROWS`: how many rows, at least 1.
- `COLUMNS`: how many columns, at least 1.
- `ROW-SPACING`: distance from one row to the next, in millimetres.
- `COLUMN-SPACING`: distance from one column to the next, in millimetres.
- `ANGLE`: direction of the columns, in radians; the rows are 90 degrees from it.

### `region-changes` (function)

```lisp
(region-changes drawing ids operation &key base-id)
```

Return the change set that joins, overlaps or cuts the closed shapes with IDS, replacing them by the polylines of
what results, on the layer of the first, and how many shapes were used and how many of IDS were skipped. Signals
EDIT-REFUSED when fewer than two shapes can be used, or when nothing is left of the result.

- `DRAWING`: the drawing.
- `IDS`: list of entity ids.
- `OPERATION`: :union, :intersection or :difference.
- `BASE-ID`: for :difference, the id of the shape the others are cut from, or NIL for the first.

### `region-target` (function)

```lisp
(region-target drawing x y radius)
```

Return the closed shape that a click picks: the smallest that holds the point, and when none does, the nearest within
RADIUS of it, or NIL.

- `DRAWING`: the drawing.
- `X`: world x of the click.
- `Y`: world y of the click.
- `RADIUS`: how far from the point, in millimetres, a shape may be and still be picked.

### `remove-block` (function)

```lisp
(remove-block drawing name)
```

Take the block called NAME out of the drawing. Signals EDIT-REFUSED when it is placed.

- `DRAWING`: the drawing.
- `NAME`: a block name.

### `remove-layer` (function)

```lisp
(remove-layer drawing id)
```

Delete an empty layer. Signals LAYER-ERROR for the default layer or a layer that still
holds entities. The caller must clear the undo history, which may refer to the layer.

- `DRAWING`: the drawing.
- `ID`: layer id.

### `remove-sheet` (function)

```lisp
(remove-sheet drawing index)
```

Take away the sheet at a position. Signals EDIT-REFUSED when there is none there.

- `DRAWING`: the drawing.
- `INDEX`: the position.

### `remove-viewport` (function)

```lisp
(remove-viewport drawing index position)
```

Take a viewport away from a sheet.

- `DRAWING`: the drawing.
- `INDEX`: the position of the sheet.
- `POSITION`: the position of the viewport on it.

### `rename-layer` (function)

```lisp
(rename-layer drawing id name)
```

Rename a layer. Signals LAYER-ERROR when NAME is empty, too long or used by another layer.

- `DRAWING`: the drawing.
- `ID`: layer id.
- `NAME`: the new name.

### `replace-sheet` (function)

```lisp
(replace-sheet drawing index sheet)
```

Put a sheet in place of the one at a position. Signals EDIT-REFUSED when there is none there.

- `DRAWING`: the drawing.
- `INDEX`: the position.
- `SHEET`: the new sheet.

### `replace-viewport` (function)

```lisp
(replace-viewport drawing index position viewport)
```

Put a viewport in place of another on a sheet.

- `DRAWING`: the drawing.
- `INDEX`: the position of the sheet.
- `POSITION`: the position of the viewport on it.
- `VIEWPORT`: the new viewport.

### `restore-drawing-state` (function)

```lisp
(restore-drawing-state drawing &key current-layer-id next-entity-id next-layer-id display-unit grid-spacing grid-visible snap-to-grid object-snap default-text-height)
```

Set the drawing-wide state read from a file. Validation errors use the same conditions as
the individual setters.

- `DRAWING`: the drawing, after its layers were restored.
- `CURRENT-LAYER-ID`: id of the current layer.
- `NEXT-ENTITY-ID`: next entity id, above every existing id.
- `NEXT-LAYER-ID`: next layer id, above every existing id.
- `DISPLAY-UNIT`: display unit keyword.
- `GRID-SPACING`: grid spacing in millimetres.
- `GRID-VISIBLE`: grid visibility.
- `SNAP-TO-GRID`: grid snap flag.
- `OBJECT-SNAP`: object snap flag.
- `DEFAULT-TEXT-HEIGHT`: default text height.

### `restore-layer` (function)

```lisp
(restore-layer drawing id name color visible locked)
```

Add a layer with an explicit id, as when loading a file. Signals LAYER-ERROR on a duplicate
id or name. Layer 0 is the default layer and replaces the one made by MAKE-DRAWING.

- `DRAWING`: the drawing.
- `ID`: layer id.
- `NAME`: layer name.
- `COLOR`: an RGB color.
- `VISIBLE`: visibility flag.
- `LOCKED`: lock flag.

### `rgb` (type)

An 8-bit-per-channel color.

- `RED`: red channel, 0 to 255.
- `GREEN`: green channel, 0 to 255.
- `BLUE`: blue channel, 0 to 255.

### `rgb-blue` (function)

```lisp
(rgb-blue instance)
```

(undocumented)

### `rgb-green` (function)

```lisp
(rgb-green instance)
```

(undocumented)

### `rgb-red` (function)

```lisp
(rgb-red instance)
```

(undocumented)

### `rotate-entities-changes` (function)

```lisp
(rotate-entities-changes drawing ids center-x center-y angle &key copy)
```

Return the change set that turns the entities with IDS about a point (or adds turned copies), and the number
skipped.

- `DRAWING`: the drawing.
- `IDS`: list of entity ids.
- `CENTER-X`: x of the center of the turn.
- `CENTER-Y`: y of the center.
- `ANGLE`: radians, counter-clockwise.
- `COPY`: true to keep the originals and add the turned copies.

### `rotate-xy` (function)

```lisp
(rotate-xy x y center-x center-y angle)
```

Return, as two values, the point (X, Y) turned about the center by an angle.

- `X`: x of the point.
- `Y`: y of the point.
- `CENTER-X`: x of the center of the turn.
- `CENTER-Y`: y of the center.
- `ANGLE`: radians, counter-clockwise.

### `scale-entities-changes` (function)

```lisp
(scale-entities-changes drawing ids center-x center-y factor &key copy)
```

Return the change set that scales the entities with IDS about a point (or adds scaled copies), and the
number skipped.

- `DRAWING`: the drawing.
- `IDS`: list of entity ids.
- `CENTER-X`: x of the center of the scaling.
- `CENTER-Y`: y of the center.
- `FACTOR`: a positive number.
- `COPY`: true to keep the originals and add the scaled copies.

### `scale-xy` (function)

```lisp
(scale-xy x y center-x center-y factor)
```

Return, as two values, the point (X, Y) moved away from the center, or toward it, by a factor.

- `X`: x of the point.
- `Y`: y of the point.
- `CENTER-X`: x of the center of the scaling.
- `CENTER-Y`: y of the center.
- `FACTOR`: the number its distance from the center is multiplied by.

### `set-current-layer` (function)

```lisp
(set-current-layer drawing id)
```

Make a layer the one that receives new entities. Signals LAYER-ERROR when it is missing.

- `DRAWING`: the drawing.
- `ID`: layer id.

### `set-default-text-height` (function)

```lisp
(set-default-text-height drawing height)
```

Set the text height used for new text and dimensions.

- `DRAWING`: the drawing.
- `HEIGHT`: height in millimetres, above the length tolerance.

### `set-display-unit` (function)

```lisp
(set-display-unit drawing unit)
```

Choose the unit in which lengths are shown and typed.

- `DRAWING`: the drawing.
- `UNIT`: a supported length unit keyword.

### `set-grid-spacing` (function)

```lisp
(set-grid-spacing drawing spacing)
```

Set the grid and snap spacing. Signals INVALID-INPUT when it is below the minimum.

- `DRAWING`: the drawing.
- `SPACING`: spacing in millimetres, at least +MINIMUM-GRID-SPACING+.

### `set-grid-visible` (function)

```lisp
(set-grid-visible drawing visible)
```

Show or hide the grid.

- `DRAWING`: the drawing.
- `VISIBLE`: true to show.

### `set-layer-locked` (function)

```lisp
(set-layer-locked drawing id locked)
```

Lock or unlock a layer.

- `DRAWING`: the drawing.
- `ID`: layer id.
- `LOCKED`: true to lock.

### `set-layer-visible` (function)

```lisp
(set-layer-visible drawing id visible)
```

Show or hide a layer.

- `DRAWING`: the drawing.
- `ID`: layer id.
- `VISIBLE`: true to show.

### `set-object-snap` (function)

```lisp
(set-object-snap drawing enabled)
```

Enable or disable snapping to entity endpoints and centers.

- `DRAWING`: the drawing.
- `ENABLED`: true to snap.

### `set-sheets` (function)

```lisp
(set-sheets drawing sheets)
```

Replace the sheets of the drawing, which counts as a change to it. Signals EDIT-REFUSED when there are too many.

- `DRAWING`: the drawing.
- `SHEETS`: the list of sheets, in order.

### `set-snap-to-grid` (function)

```lisp
(set-snap-to-grid drawing enabled)
```

Enable or disable snapping to the grid.

- `DRAWING`: the drawing.
- `ENABLED`: true to snap.

### `sheet` (type)

A page that is printed.

- `NAME`: what it is called in the list of sheets.
- `PAPER`: a keyword of *SHEET-PAPERS*.
- `LANDSCAPE`: true when the paper lies, with its long side across.
- `FIELDS`: the title block, as an alist from the keys of *TITLE-FIELD-KEYS* to text, with every key in it.
- `VIEWPORTS`: the viewports, the first at the bottom.

### `sheet-at` (function)

```lisp
(sheet-at drawing index)
```

Return the sheet at a position, from 0, or NIL when there is none.

- `DRAWING`: the drawing.
- `INDEX`: its position.

### `sheet-count` (function)

```lisp
(sheet-count drawing)
```

Return how many sheets the drawing has.

- `DRAWING`: the drawing.

### `sheet-field` (function)

```lisp
(sheet-field sheet key)
```

Return what a field of the title block says.

- `SHEET`: the sheet.
- `KEY`: a key of *TITLE-FIELD-KEYS*.

### `sheet-fields` (function)

```lisp
(sheet-fields instance)
```

(undocumented)

### `sheet-landscape` (function)

```lisp
(sheet-landscape instance)
```

(undocumented)

### `sheet-name` (function)

```lisp
(sheet-name instance)
```

(undocumented)

### `sheet-p` (function)

```lisp
(sheet-p object)
```

(undocumented)

### `sheet-paper` (function)

```lisp
(sheet-paper instance)
```

(undocumented)

### `sheet-viewports` (function)

```lisp
(sheet-viewports instance)
```

(undocumented)

### `sheet-with` (function)

```lisp
(sheet-with sheet &key (name (sheet-name sheet)) (paper (sheet-paper sheet)) (landscape (sheet-landscape sheet)) (fields (sheet-fields sheet)) (viewports (sheet-viewports sheet)))
```

Return a sheet like another with some of it changed.

- `SHEET`: the sheet.
- `NAME`: the new name.
- `PAPER`: the new paper.
- `LANDSCAPE`: whether the paper lies.
- `FIELDS`: the new title block, in whole, or the old one with some fields changed when it is an alist.
- `VIEWPORTS`: the new list of viewports.

### `snap-point` (function)

```lisp
(snap-point drawing x y object-radius &optional anchor)
```

Return the snapped position for a pointer at (X, Y) as three values (x, y, kind). The kind
is one of the kinds of object snap (see FIND-OBJECT-SNAP), :grid for grid snap, NIL when nothing
snapped. Object snap wins over grid snap; either applies only when enabled in the drawing.

- `DRAWING`: the drawing.
- `X`: pointer x in millimetres.
- `Y`: pointer y in millimetres.
- `OBJECT-RADIUS`: object-snap radius in millimetres.
- `ANCHOR`: an (x . y) cons, the last point drawn, or NIL.

### `snap-to-grid` (function)

```lisp
(snap-to-grid drawing x y)
```

Return the grid point nearest to (X, Y) as two values.

- `DRAWING`: the drawing, which supplies the grid spacing.
- `X`: point x in millimetres.
- `Y`: point y in millimetres.

### `spread-changes` (function)

```lisp
(spread-changes drawing id distance)
```

Return the change set that takes the entity with ID apart and moves each piece DISTANCE away from the others,
the pieces on its layer. Signals EDIT-REFUSED when the entity is missing, on a locked layer or of a kind that
cannot be spread, when it is a single piece, or when the distance is not above zero, and INVALID-ENTITY when a
piece would not be valid.

- `DRAWING`: the drawing.
- `ID`: id of the entity to spread.
- `DISTANCE`: millimetres.

### `spread-selection-changes` (function)

```lisp
(spread-selection-changes drawing ids distance)
```

Return the change set that takes apart every entity among IDS that can be spread and moves its pieces DISTANCE from
each other, how many entities were spread, and how many of IDS were skipped (missing, on a locked layer, or of a
kind with no pieces). Signals EDIT-REFUSED when the distance is not above zero.

- `DRAWING`: the drawing.
- `IDS`: list of entity ids.
- `DISTANCE`: millimetres.

### `spread-target` (function)

```lisp
(spread-target drawing x y radius)
```

Return the entity that a click spreads, or NIL: the smallest closed shape, or block, that holds the point, and
when none does, the entity that can be spread nearest to it within RADIUS.

- `DRAWING`: the drawing.
- `X`: world x of the click.
- `Y`: world y of the click.
- `RADIUS`: how far from the point, in millimetres, an entity may be and still be picked.

### `store-add` (function)

```lisp
(store-add store entity)
```

Insert ENTITY at the position its id dictates. Signals an error when the id exists.

- `STORE`: an entity store.
- `ENTITY`: the entity to add.

### `store-add-many` (function)

```lisp
(store-add-many store entities)
```

Insert many entities in a single merge. Signals an error, before changing anything, when
an id already exists or is repeated.

- `STORE`: an entity store.
- `ENTITIES`: list of entities to add.

### `store-count` (function)

```lisp
(store-count instance)
```

(undocumented)

### `store-entity-at` (function)

```lisp
(store-entity-at store index)
```

Return the INDEX-th entity in id order.

- `STORE`: an entity store.
- `INDEX`: position from 0 below the count.

### `store-find` (function)

```lisp
(store-find store id)
```

Return the entity with ID, or NIL.

- `STORE`: an entity store.
- `ID`: entity id.

### `store-remove` (function)

```lisp
(store-remove store id)
```

Remove and return the entity with ID. Signals an error when absent.

- `STORE`: an entity store.
- `ID`: entity id.

### `store-remove-many` (function)

```lisp
(store-remove-many store ids)
```

Remove every entity whose id is in IDS in a single pass; return the removed entities.
Signals an error, before changing anything, when an id is absent.

- `STORE`: an entity store.
- `IDS`: list of entity ids.

### `store-replace` (function)

```lisp
(store-replace store entity)
```

Replace the stored entity that has the same id as ENTITY. Signals an error when absent.

- `STORE`: an entity store.
- `ENTITY`: the new value.

### `text-angle` (function)

```lisp
(text-angle instance)
```

(undocumented)

### `text-entity` (type)

A single-line text annotation laid out on a fixed-advance grid (see geometry text extents).

- `X`: x of the baseline start (insertion point).
- `Y`: y of the baseline start.
- `STRING`: the text, one character cell per code point.
- `HEIGHT`: text height (em size) in millimetres.
- `ANGLE`: direction of the baseline in radians, counter-clockwise from +x, in [0, 2 pi).

### `text-height` (function)

```lisp
(text-height instance)
```

(undocumented)

### `text-string` (function)

```lisp
(text-string instance)
```

(undocumented)

### `text-x` (function)

```lisp
(text-x instance)
```

(undocumented)

### `text-y` (function)

```lisp
(text-y instance)
```

(undocumented)

### `transform-entities-changes` (function)

```lisp
(transform-entities-changes drawing ids transform copy)
```

Return the change set that replaces the entities with IDS by what TRANSFORM makes of them, or, when COPY
is true, adds what it makes of them as new entities and leaves them as they are, and the number skipped
because they are missing or on a locked layer. Signals INVALID-ENTITY when a result would not be valid.

- `DRAWING`: the drawing.
- `IDS`: list of entity ids.
- `TRANSFORM`: function of an entity that returns the transformed entity, with the same id and layer.
- `COPY`: true to keep the originals and add the results.

### `translate-entities-changes` (function)

```lisp
(translate-entities-changes drawing ids dx dy)
```

Return the change set that moves the entities with IDS by (DX, DY), and the number skipped.
Signals INVALID-ENTITY when a moved entity would leave the drawing limits.

- `DRAWING`: the drawing.
- `IDS`: list of entity ids.
- `DX`: displacement along x in millimetres.
- `DY`: displacement along y in millimetres.

### `trim-changes` (function)

```lisp
(trim-changes drawing id x y)
```

Return the change set that trims the line, arc or circle with ID: the part of it that lies between the two
places where other entities cross it, or between one and its end, and that holds the point, is cut away. A line
or an arc may be left in two pieces. Signals EDIT-REFUSED when it cannot be done.

- `DRAWING`: the drawing.
- `ID`: id of the entity to trim.
- `X`: world x of the click, which says which part to cut away.
- `Y`: world y of the click.

### `update-angular-dimension` (function)

```lisp
(update-angular-dimension dimension &key (id (entity-id dimension)) (layer-id (entity-layer-id dimension)) (vertex-x (angular-dimension-vertex-x dimension)) (vertex-y (angular-dimension-vertex-y dimension)) (start-angle (angular-dimension-start-angle dimension)) (end-angle (angular-dimension-end-angle dimension)) (radius (angular-dimension-radius dimension)) (text-height (angular-dimension-text-height dimension)) (precision (angular-dimension-precision dimension)))
```

Return a validated copy of DIMENSION with the given fields replaced.

- `DIMENSION`: the dimension to copy.
- `ID`: replacement id.
- `LAYER-ID`: replacement layer id.
- `VERTEX-X`: replacement vertex x.
- `VERTEX-Y`: replacement vertex y.
- `START-ANGLE`: replacement direction of the first arm.
- `END-ANGLE`: replacement direction of the second arm.
- `RADIUS`: replacement arc radius.
- `TEXT-HEIGHT`: replacement text height.
- `PRECISION`: replacement decimals.

### `update-arc` (function)

```lisp
(update-arc arc &key (id (entity-id arc)) (layer-id (entity-layer-id arc)) (center-x (arc-center-x arc)) (center-y (arc-center-y arc)) (radius (arc-radius arc)) (start-angle (arc-start-angle arc)) (end-angle (arc-end-angle arc)))
```

Return a validated copy of ARC with the given fields replaced.

- `ARC`: the arc to copy.
- `ID`: replacement id.
- `LAYER-ID`: replacement layer id.
- `CENTER-X`: replacement center x.
- `CENTER-Y`: replacement center y.
- `RADIUS`: replacement radius.
- `START-ANGLE`: replacement start angle.
- `END-ANGLE`: replacement end angle.

### `update-circle` (function)

```lisp
(update-circle circle &key (id (entity-id circle)) (layer-id (entity-layer-id circle)) (center-x (circle-center-x circle)) (center-y (circle-center-y circle)) (radius (circle-radius circle)))
```

Return a validated copy of CIRCLE with the given fields replaced.

- `CIRCLE`: the circle to copy.
- `ID`: replacement id.
- `LAYER-ID`: replacement layer id.
- `CENTER-X`: replacement center x.
- `CENTER-Y`: replacement center y.
- `RADIUS`: replacement radius.

### `update-dimension` (function)

```lisp
(update-dimension dimension &key (id (entity-id dimension)) (layer-id (entity-layer-id dimension)) (x1 (dimension-x1 dimension)) (y1 (dimension-y1 dimension)) (x2 (dimension-x2 dimension)) (y2 (dimension-y2 dimension)) (offset (dimension-offset dimension)) (text-height (dimension-text-height dimension)) (unit (dimension-unit dimension)) (precision (dimension-precision dimension)))
```

Return a validated copy of DIMENSION with the given fields replaced.

- `DIMENSION`: the dimension to copy.
- `ID`: replacement id.
- `LAYER-ID`: replacement layer id.
- `X1`: replacement x of the first point.
- `Y1`: replacement y of the first point.
- `X2`: replacement x of the second point.
- `Y2`: replacement y of the second point.
- `OFFSET`: replacement offset.
- `TEXT-HEIGHT`: replacement text height.
- `UNIT`: replacement display unit.
- `PRECISION`: replacement decimals.

```lisp
(update-ellipse ellipse &key (id (entity-id ellipse)) (layer-id (entity-layer-id ellipse)) (center-x (ellipse-center-x ellipse)) (center-y (ellipse-center-y ellipse)) (radius-x (ellipse-radius-x ellipse)) (radius-y (ellipse-radius-y ellipse)) (rotation (ellipse-rotation ellipse)))
```

- `CENTER-Y`: replacement center y.

### `update-hatch` (function)

```lisp
(update-hatch hatch &key (id (entity-id hatch)) (layer-id (entity-layer-id hatch)) (coordinates (hatch-coordinates hatch)) (holes (hatch-holes hatch)) (pattern (hatch-pattern hatch)) (angle (hatch-angle hatch)) (spacing (hatch-spacing hatch)))
```

### `update-insert` (function)

```lisp
(update-insert insert &key (id (entity-id insert)) (layer-id (entity-layer-id insert)) (block (insert-block insert)) (x (insert-x insert)) (y (insert-y insert)) (scale (insert-scale insert)) (rotation (insert-rotation insert)) (flipped (insert-flipped insert)))

```

### `valid-grid-spacing-p` (function)

```lisp
(valid-grid-spacing-p value)
```

Return true when VALUE is an acceptable grid spacing in millimetres.

- `VALUE`: any object.

### `viewport` (type)

A window on the drawing, put on a sheet.

- `X`: x of its lower left corner on the sheet, in millimetres from the sheet's lower left corner.
- `Y`: y of that corner.
- `WIDTH`: width on the sheet.
- `HEIGHT`: height on the sheet.
- `CENTER-X`: x of the point of the drawing at the center of the window.
- `CENTER-Y`: y of that point.
- `SCALE`: millimetres of paper for a millimetre of the drawing.
